the Lake,prow weary and fo get out,or elfe the

caofe it has not been fo exaétly done before, as 
in Regard that the Settlement which is going

Way, thofe who perform the Voyage may have 
the Conveniency of taking in Refrefhments

ther, and of careening or refitting. As for the 
Veracruz, tho’ farther out of the Way, the 
Correfpondence there may be advantagious for 
the Securing of the Colony of Lousiana, 

But how can that fail of fucceeding, under 
the Condu& of Monfieur Crozat, who has the 
Charge of that Enterprize, and whom Provi- 
dence feems to have in a Manner ingap’d to ad- 
vance in Wealth and Honour, tothe Amaze- 
meat of the World, and yet free from Envy, 
from Jealoufy, and from any Sort of Complaints.

Bleflings God has pour’d down upon all his

for what is to follow. There is Reafon tohope |
